# wsbench_chat claim map (taxonomy v2) — judge claude-opus-5-5 — PRE-GATE

**PRE-GATE**: the held-out calibration gate has not passed for this prompt version; these numbers are provisional.

Mode: single-call. Shares in % of readout characters (per-readout shares averaged over readouts; each readout weighs the same); item-bootstrap 95% CI in brackets (2,000 draws, seed 0). The 6 verdicts + `failed` sum to 100, and so do the 7 groups + `unknown_incorrect` (anchored runs only: established v5c spans the judge did not label incorrect, or disputed) + `failed`. Readout-level rates are over readouts of successfully mapped cells: `all` = every non-empty readout, `spec` = v5c-specific readouts (plan 0001's denominator). A readout-level verdict rate is the share of readouts with at least one segment of that verdict. Rev 19: hedged option lists are scored `unverifiable` (`same_slot_alternative`), and `model_state` is the `thinky` verdict. Rev 18: `junk` merges the judge's `non_claim` and `off_topic` after parsing (the `reason` tag keeps the kind; see the junk-by-reason table).

Verdicts: `hallucination` = detail, false_claim; `probe_candidate` = substitution_sibling, substitution_category, supplementary_false, ambiguous_topic; `thinky` = model_state; `correct` = correct, supplementary_true; `unresolved` = unverifiable, unknown_incorrect; `junk` = junk
